   621→// BranchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.
   622→func (env *TestEnv) BranchExists(branchName string) bool {
   623→	env.T.Helper()
   624→
   625→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   626→	if err != nil {
   627→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	refs, err := repo.References()
   631→	if err != nil {
   632→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	found := false
   636→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   637→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   638→		if ref.Name().Short() == branchName {
   639→			found = true
   640→		}
   641→		return nil
   642→	})
   643→
   644→	return found
   645→}
   646→
   647→// ListBranchesWithPrefix returns all branches that start with the given prefix.
   648→func (env *TestEnv) ListBranchesWithPrefix(prefix string) []string {
   649→	env.T.Helper()
   650→
   651→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   652→	if err != nil {
   653→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	refs, err := repo.References()
   657→	if err != nil {
   658→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   659→	}
   660→
   661→	var branches []string
   662→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   663→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   664→		name := ref.Name().Short()
   665→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   666→			branches = append(branches, name)
   667→		}
   668→		return nil
   669→	})
   670→
   671→	return branches
   672→}

   674→// GetCommitMessage returns the commit message for the given commit hash.
   675→func (env *TestEnv) GetCommitMessage(hash string) string {
   676→	env.T.Helper()
   677→
   678→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   679→	if err != nil {
   680→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(hash)
   684→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   685→	if err != nil {
   686→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get commit %s: %v", hash, err)
   687→	}
   688→
   689→	return commit.Message
   690→}
   691→
   692→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   693→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   694→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   695→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   696→	env.T.Helper()
   697→
   698→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   699→	if err != nil {
   700→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   701→	}
   702→
   703→	head, err := repo.Head()
   704→	if err != nil {
   705→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   709→	if err != nil {
   710→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	var checkpointID string
   714→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   715→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   716→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   717→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   718→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   719→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   720→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   721→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   722→			}
   723→		}
   724→		return nil
   725→	})
   726→
   727→	if checkpointID == "" {
   728→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   729→	}
   730→
   731→	return checkpointID, nil
   732→}
   733→
   734→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   735→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   736→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   737→	env.T.Helper()
   738→
   739→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   740→	if err != nil {
   741→		return ""
   742→	}
   743→
   744→	head, err := repo.Head()
   745→	if err != nil {
   746→		return ""
   747→	}
   748→
   749→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   750→	if err != nil {
   751→		return ""
   752→	}
   753→
   754→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   755→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   756→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   757→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   758→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   759→		}
   760→	}
   761→	return ""
   762→}

   764→// safeIDPrefix returns first 12 chars of ID or the full ID if shorter.
   765→// Use this when logging checkpoint IDs to avoid index out of bounds panic.
   766→func safeIDPrefix(id string) string {
   767→	if len(id) >= 12 {
   768→		return id[:12]
   769→	}
   770→	return id
   771→}

   941→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   942→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   943→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   944→	env.T.Helper()
   945→
   946→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   947→	if err != nil {
   948→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   949→	}
   950→
   951→	// Get the branch reference
   952→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   953→	if err != nil {
   954→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   955→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   956→		if err != nil {
   957→			return "", false
   958→		}
   959→	}
   960→
   961→	// Get the commit
   962→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   963→	if err != nil {
   964→		return "", false
   965→	}
   966→
   967→	// Get the tree
   968→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   969→	if err != nil {
   970→		return "", false
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	// Get the file
   974→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   975→	if err != nil {
   976→		return "", false
   977→	}
   978→
   979→	// Get the content
   980→	content, err := file.Contents()
   981→	if err != nil {
   982→		return "", false
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	return content, true
   986→}

   988→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
   989→type CheckpointValidation struct {
   990→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
   991→	CheckpointID string
   992→
   993→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name (optional)
   994→	Strategy string
   995→
   996→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched (optional)
   997→	FilesTouched []string
   998→
   999→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt (optional)
  1000→	ExpectedPrompts []string
  1001→
  1002→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl (optional)
  1003→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
  1004→}
  1005→
  1006→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
  1007→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1008→	env.T.Helper()
  1009→
  1010→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
  1011→
  1012→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1013→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1014→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1015→	}
  1016→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1017→
  1018→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1019→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1020→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1021→	if !found {
  1022→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1023→	} else {
  1024→		var summary map[string]any
  1025→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1026→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1027→		} else {
  1028→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1029→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1030→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1031→			}
  1032→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1033→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1034→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1035→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1036→				}
  1037→			}
  1038→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1039→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1040→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1041→			}
  1042→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1043→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1044→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1045→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1046→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1047→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1048→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1049→						}
  1050→					}
  1051→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1052→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1053→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1054→						}
  1055→					}
  1056→				}
  1057→			}
  1058→		}
  1059→	}
  1060→
  1061→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1062→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1063→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1064→	if !found {
  1065→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1066→	} else {
  1067→		var metadata map[string]any
  1068→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1069→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1070→		} else {
  1071→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1072→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1073→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1074→			}
  1075→			// Validate created_at exists
  1076→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1077→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1078→			}
  1079→		}
  1080→	}
  1081→
  1082→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1083→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1084→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1085→	if !found {
  1086→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1087→	} else {
  1088→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1089→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1090→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1091→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1092→		validLines := 0
  1093→		for i, line := range lines {
  1094→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1095→			if line == "" {
  1096→				continue
  1097→			}
  1098→			validLines++
  1099→			var obj map[string]any
  1100→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1101→				if isClaudeCode {
  1102→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1103→				}
  1104→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1105→			}
  1106→		}
  1107→		if validLines == 0 {
  1108→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1109→		}
  1110→		// Check expected content
  1111→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1112→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1113→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1114→			}
  1115→		}
  1116→	}
  1117→
  1118→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1119→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1120→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1121→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1122→		if !found {
  1123→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1124→		} else {
  1125→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1126→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1127→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1128→				}
  1129→			}
  1130→		}
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1134→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1135→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1136→	if !found {
  1137→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1138→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1139→		// Verify hash matches
  1140→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1141→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1142→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1143→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1144→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1145→		}
  1146→	}
  1147→}
  1148→

    27→// getPluginPath returns the absolute path to the plugin file.
    28→func getPluginPath() (string, error) {
    29→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    32→		//nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    33→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd()
    34→		if err != nil {
    35→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    36→		}
    37→	}
    38→	return filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".opencode", pluginDirName, pluginFileName), nil
    39→}
    40→
    41→// InstallHooks writes the Entire plugin file to .opencode/plugins/entire.ts.
    42→// Returns 1 if the plugin was installed, 0 if already present (idempotent).
    43→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    44→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return 0, err
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Check if already installed (idempotent) unless force
    50→	if !force {
    51→		if _, err := os.Stat(pluginPath); err == nil {
    52→			data, readErr := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
    53→			if readErr == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker) {
    54→				return 0, nil // Already installed
    55→			}
    56→		}
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Build the command prefix
    60→	var cmdPrefix string
    61→	if localDev {
    62→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${OPENCODE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go"
    63→	} else {
    64→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Generate plugin content from template
    68→	content := strings.ReplaceAll(pluginTemplate, entireCmdPlaceholder, cmdPrefix)
    69→
    70→	// Ensure directory exists
    71→	pluginDir := filepath.Dir(pluginPath)
    72→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Plugin directory needs standard permissions
    73→	if err := os.MkdirAll(pluginDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    74→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create plugin directory: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Write plugin file
    78→	//nolint:gosec // G306: Plugin file needs standard permissions for OpenCode to read
    79→	if err := os.WriteFile(pluginPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
    80→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write plugin file: %w", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	return 1, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// UninstallHooks removes the Entire plugin file.
    87→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
    88→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return err
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	if err := os.Remove(pluginPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    94→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove plugin file: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	return nil
    98→}
    99→
   100→// AreHooksInstalled checks if the Entire plugin file exists and contains the marker.
   101→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   102→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return false
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	data, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return false
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	return strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// GetSupportedHooks returns the normalized lifecycle events this agent supports.
   116→// OpenCode's native hooks map to standard agent lifecycle events:
   117→//   - session-start → HookSessionStart
   118→//   - session-end   → HookSessionEnd
   119→//   - turn-start    → HookUserPromptSubmit (user prompt triggers a turn)
   120→//   - turn-end      → HookStop (agent response complete)
   121→//
   122→// Note: HookNames() returns 5 hooks (including "compaction"), but GetSupportedHooks()
   123→// returns only 4. The "compaction" hook is OpenCode-specific with no standard HookType
   124→// mapping — it is handled via ParseHookEvent but not advertised as a standard lifecycle event.
   125→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   126→	return []agent.HookType{
   127→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   128→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   129→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   130→		agent.HookStop,
   131→	}
   132→}
   133→

    35→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    36→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    37→	switch hookName {
    38→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    39→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    40→		if err != nil {
    41→			return nil, err
    42→		}
    43→		return &agent.Event{
    44→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    45→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    46→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    47→		}, nil
    48→
    49→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    50→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    51→		if err != nil {
    52→			return nil, err
    53→		}
    54→		// Get the temp file path for this session (may not exist yet, but needed for pre-prompt state).
    55→		repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    56→		if err != nil {
    57→			repoRoot = "."
    58→		}
    59→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    60→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    61→		return &agent.Event{
    62→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    63→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    64→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    65→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    66→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    67→		}, nil
    68→
    69→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    70→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    71→		if err != nil {
    72→			return nil, err
    73→		}
    74→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    75→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    76→		if exportErr != nil {
    77→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    78→		}
    79→		return &agent.Event{
    80→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    81→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    82→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    83→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    84→		}, nil
    85→
    86→	case HookNameCompaction:
    87→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			return nil, err
    90→		}
    91→		return &agent.Event{
    92→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    93→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    94→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    95→		}, nil
    96→
    97→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    98→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    99→		if err != nil {
   100→			return nil, err
   101→		}
   102→		return &agent.Event{
   103→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   104→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   105→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   106→		}, nil
   107→
   108→	default:
   109→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   110→	}
   111→}
   112→
   113→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   114→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   115→//
   116→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   117→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   118→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   119→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   120→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   121→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   122→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		repoRoot = "."
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   128→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   129→
   130→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   131→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   132→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   133→			return tmpFile, nil
   134→		}
   135→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   139→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Validate output is valid JSON before caching
   145→	if !json.Valid(data) {
   146→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export returned invalid JSON (%d bytes)", len(data))
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   150→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   151→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   155→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	return tmpFile, nil
   159→}

Based on my research, here's what I found about OpenCode's exit behavior and Bun.spawnSync:

## OpenCode Run Command Exit Behavior

### 1. **Does it call `process.exit()` immediately?**

No, not in normal operation. The OpenCode `run.ts` command uses a **break statement** to exit the event loop gracefully:

```typescript
if (
  event.type === "session.status" &&
  event.properties.sessionID === sessionID &&
  event.properties.status.type === "idle"
) {
  break  // Exits the async loop when session becomes idle
}
```

The command detects when `session.status` becomes `"idle"` and simply breaks out of the event listening loop, allowing the handler to complete naturally.

### 2. **Does it await anything before exiting?**

Yes, the implementation is event-driven and async:
- It listens to `session.status` events from the SDK event stream
- It awaits these events until the idle status is detected
- The break statement exits the listening loop gracefully
- The handler then completes normally without explicit cleanup

### 3. **Is there a cleanup phase?**

The code only calls `process.exit(1)` in **error scenarios**:
- Uncaught exceptions in the event loop: `console.error(e); process.exit(1)`
- Validation failures (missing messages, invalid flags, file access issues)

Normal completion is graceful without forced exit.

## Bun.spawnSync Child Process Behavior

According to Bun documentation:

**The parent Bun process will NOT exit until all child processes have exited.** By default:
- `Bun.spawnSync` is a blocking API that waits for the spawned process to complete
- The parent process will **wait** for all spawned children to finish naturally
- To allow a child to survive parent exit, you would need to call `proc.unref()` on the subprocess instance to detach it

This means if you spawn a process with `Bun.spawnSync` without calling `unref()`, the parent will block waiting for that child to complete before the parent can exit.

## Key Implication

If OpenCode's `run` command uses `Bun.spawnSync` to spawn a subprocess (like a git hook process), the spawned process **will not survive** if the parent OpenCode process exits — the parent will wait for the child to complete first.

Sources:
- [Bun Spawn Documentation](https://bun.com/docs/runtime/child-process)
- [Bun.spawnSync API Reference](https://bun.com/reference/bun/spawnSync)
- [OpenCode Run Command Documentation](https://opencode.ai/docs/cli/)
- [OpenCode GitHub Repository](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode)

> AGENT

Good news from the research: OpenCode's `run` command exits **gracefully** (via `break` from the event loop), not via `process.exit()`. And `Bun.spawnSync` **blocks the parent process** until the child completes. This means a synchronous hook call should work — the OpenCode process can't exit until our sync hook finishes.

To answer your question: `session.status` **does fire** (we confirmed that in the debug logs). The problem is that the plugin's `callHook` is **async** — it spawns a child process with `await $`...`` but the `opencode run` event loop breaks on the same `session.status idle` event before the plugin's async handler resolves. The async child process gets killed as the parent exits.

The fix is to make the turn-end hook **synchronous** using `Bun.spawnSync` so it blocks the event loop and completes before `opencode run` can exit.

     1→// Entire CLI plugin for OpenCode
     2→// Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`
     3→// Do not edit manually — changes will be overwritten on next install.
     4→// Requires Bun runtime (used by OpenCode's plugin system for loading ESM plugins).
     5→import type { Plugin } from "@opencode-ai/plugin"
     6→
     7→export const EntirePlugin: Plugin = async ({ $, directory }) => {
     8→  const ENTIRE_CMD = "__ENTIRE_CMD__"
     9→  // Track seen user messages to fire turn-start only once per message
    10→  const seenUserMessages = new Set<string>()
    11→  // Track current session ID for message events (which don't include sessionID)
    12→  let currentSessionID: string | null = null
    13→  // In-memory store for message metadata (role, tokens, etc.)
    14→  const messageStore = new Map<string, any>()
    15→
    16→  /**
    17→   * Pipe JSON payload to an entire hooks command.
    18→   * Errors are logged but never thrown — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode.
    19→   */
    20→  async function callHook(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    21→    try {
    22→      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)
    23→      await $`echo ${json} | ${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`.quiet().nothrow()
    24→    } catch {
    25→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
    26→    }
    27→  }
    28→
    29→  return {
    30→    event: async ({ event }) => {
    31→      switch (event.type) {
    32→        case "session.created": {
    33→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
    34→          if (!session?.id) break
    35→          // Reset per-session tracking state when switching sessions.
    36→          if (currentSessionID !== session.id) {
    37→            seenUserMessages.clear()
    38→            messageStore.clear()
    39→          }
    40→          currentSessionID = session.id
    41→          await callHook("session-start", {
    42→            session_id: session.id,
    43→          })
    44→          break
    45→        }
    46→
    47→        case "message.updated": {
    48→          const msg = (event as any).properties?.info
    49→          if (!msg) break
    50→          // Store message metadata (role, time, tokens, etc.)
    51→          messageStore.set(msg.id, msg)
    52→          break
    53→        }
    54→
    55→        case "message.part.updated": {
    56→          const part = (event as any).properties?.part
    57→          if (!part?.messageID) break
    58→
    59→          // Fire turn-start on the first text part of a new user message
    60→          const msg = messageStore.get(part.messageID)
    61→          if (msg?.role === "user" && part.type === "text" && !seenUserMessages.has(msg.id)) {
    62→            seenUserMessages.add(msg.id)
    63→            const sessionID = msg.sessionID ?? currentSessionID
    64→            if (sessionID) {
    65→              await callHook("turn-start", {
    66→                session_id: sessionID,
    67→                prompt: part.text ?? "",
    68→              })
    69→            }
    70→          }
    71→          break
    72→        }
    73→
    74→        case "session.status": {
    75→          // session.status fires in both TUI and non-interactive (run) mode.
    76→          // session.idle is deprecated and not reliably emitted in run mode.
    77→          const props = (event as any).properties
    78→          if (props?.status?.type !== "idle") break
    79→          const sessionID = props?.sessionID
    80→          if (!sessionID) break
    81→          // Go hook handler will call `opencode export` to get the transcript
    82→          await callHook("turn-end", {
    83→            session_id: sessionID,
    84→          })
    85→          break
    86→        }
    87→
    88→        case "session.compacted": {
    89→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
    90→          if (!sessionID) break
    91→          await callHook("compaction", {
    92→            session_id: sessionID,
    93→          })
    94→          break
    95→        }
    96→
    97→        case "session.deleted": {
    98→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
    99→          if (!session?.id) break
   100→          seenUserMessages.clear()
   101→          messageStore.clear()
   102→          currentSessionID = null
   103→          await callHook("session-end", {
   104→            session_id: session.id,
   105→          })
   106→          break
   107→        }
   108→      }
   109→    },
   110→  }
   111→}
   112→
```

  "new_string": "  /**\n   * Pipe JSON payload to an entire hooks command (async).\n   * Errors are logged but never thrown — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode.\n   */\n  async function callHook(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {\n    try {\n      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)\n      await $`echo ${json} | ${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`.quiet().nothrow()\n    } catch {\n      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Synchronous variant for hooks that fire near process exit (turn-end, session-end).\n   * `opencode run` breaks its event loop on the same session.status idle event that\n   * triggers turn-end. The async callHook would be killed before completing.\n   * Bun.spawnSync blocks the event loop, preventing exit until the hook finishes.\n   */\n  function callHookSync(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {\n    try {\n      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)\n      Bun.spawnSync([\"sh\", \"-c\", `${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`], {\n        cwd: directory,\n        stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(json + \"\\n\"),\n        stdout: \"ignore\",\n        stderr: \"ignore\",\n      })\n    } catch {\n      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode\n    }\n  }"
}

     1→// Entire CLI plugin for OpenCode
     2→// Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`
     3→// Do not edit manually — changes will be overwritten on next install.
     4→// Requires Bun runtime (used by OpenCode's plugin system for loading ESM plugins).
     5→import type { Plugin } from "@opencode-ai/plugin"
     6→
     7→export const EntirePlugin: Plugin = async ({ $, directory }) => {
     8→  const ENTIRE_CMD = "__ENTIRE_CMD__"
     9→  // Track seen user messages to fire turn-start only once per message
    10→  const seenUserMessages = new Set<string>()
    11→  // Track current session ID for message events (which don't include sessionID)
    12→  let currentSessionID: string | null = null
    13→  // In-memory store for message metadata (role, tokens, etc.)
    14→  const messageStore = new Map<string, any>()
    15→
    16→  /**
    17→   * Pipe JSON payload to an entire hooks command (async).
    18→   * Errors are logged but never thrown — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode.
    19→   */
    20→  async function callHook(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    21→    try {
    22→      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)
    23→      await $`echo ${json} | ${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`.quiet().nothrow()
    24→    } catch {
    25→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
    26→    }
    27→  }
    28→
    29→  /**
    30→   * Synchronous variant for hooks that fire near process exit (turn-end, session-end).
    31→   * `opencode run` breaks its event loop on the same session.status idle event that
    32→   * triggers turn-end. The async callHook would be killed before completing.
    33→   * Bun.spawnSync blocks the event loop, preventing exit until the hook finishes.
    34→   */
    35→  function callHookSync(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    36→    try {
    37→      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)
    38→      Bun.spawnSync(["sh", "-c", `${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`], {
    39→        cwd: directory,
    40→        stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(json + "\n"),
    41→        stdout: "ignore",
    42→        stderr: "ignore",
    43→      })
    44→    } catch {
    45→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
    46→    }
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  return {
    50→    event: async ({ event }) => {
    51→      switch (event.type) {
    52→        case "session.created": {
    53→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
    54→          if (!session?.id) break
    55→          // Reset per-session tracking state when switching sessions.
    56→          if (currentSessionID !== session.id) {
    57→            seenUserMessages.clear()
    58→            messageStore.clear()
    59→          }
    60→          currentSessionID = session.id
    61→          await callHook("session-start", {
    62→            session_id: session.id,
    63→          })
    64→          break
    65→        }
    66→
    67→        case "message.updated": {
    68→          const msg = (event as any).properties?.info
    69→          if (!msg) break
    70→          // Store message metadata (role, time, tokens, etc.)
    71→          messageStore.set(msg.id, msg)
    72→          break
    73→        }
    74→
    75→        case "message.part.updated": {
    76→          const part = (event as any).properties?.part
    77→          if (!part?.messageID) break
    78→
    79→          // Fire turn-start on the first text part of a new user message
    80→          const msg = messageStore.get(part.messageID)
    81→          if (msg?.role === "user" && part.type === "text" && !seenUserMessages.has(msg.id)) {
    82→            seenUserMessages.add(msg.id)
    83→            const sessionID = msg.sessionID ?? currentSessionID
    84→            if (sessionID) {
    85→              await callHook("turn-start", {
    86→                session_id: sessionID,
    87→                prompt: part.text ?? "",
    88→              })
    89→            }
    90→          }
    91→          break
    92→        }
    93→
    94→        case "session.status": {
    95→          // session.status fires in both TUI and non-interactive (run) mode.
    96→          // session.idle is deprecated and not reliably emitted in run mode.
    97→          const props = (event as any).properties
    98→          if (props?.status?.type !== "idle") break
    99→          const sessionID = props?.sessionID
   100→          if (!sessionID) break
   101→          // Use sync variant: `opencode run` exits on the same idle event,
   102→          // so an async hook would be killed before completing.
   103→          callHookSync("turn-end", {
   104→            session_id: sessionID,
   105→          })
   106→          break
   107→        }
   108→
   109→        case "session.compacted": {
   110→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
   111→          if (!sessionID) break
   112→          await callHook("compaction", {
   113→            session_id: sessionID,
   114→          })
   115→          break
   116→        }
   117→
   118→        case "session.deleted": {
   119→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
   120→          if (!session?.id) break
   121→          seenUserMessages.clear()
   122→          messageStore.clear()
   123→          currentSessionID = null
   124→          // Use sync variant: session-end may fire during shutdown.
   125→          callHookSync("session-end", {
   126→            session_id: session.id,
   127→          })
   128→          break
   129→        }
   130→      }
   131→    },
   132→  }
   133→}
   134→
```

 	result.ExitCode = 0

 }
+
+// OpenCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for OpenCode.
+// See: https://opencode.ai/docs/cli/
+type OpenCodeRunner struct {
+	Model   string
+	Timeout time.Duration
+}
+
+// NewOpenCodeRunner creates a new OpenCode runner with the given config.
+func NewOpenCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *OpenCodeRunner {
+	model := config.Model
+	if model == "" {
+		model = os.Getenv("E2E_OPENCODE_MODEL")
+		// No default model - OpenCode uses whatever is configured in the project
+	}
+
+	timeout := config.Timeout
+	if timeout == 0 {
+		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
+			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
+				timeout = parsed
+			}
+		}
+		if timeout == 0 {
+			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
+		}
+	}
+
+	return &OpenCodeRunner{
+		Model:   model,
+		Timeout: timeout,
+	}
+}
+
+func (r *OpenCodeRunner) Name() string {
+	return AgentNameOpenCode
+}
+
+// IsAvailable checks if OpenCode CLI is installed and responds to --version.
+func (r *OpenCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
+	if _, err := exec.LookPath("opencode"); err != nil {
+		return false, fmt.Errorf("opencode CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
+	defer cancel()
+
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "--version")
+	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
+		return false, fmt.Errorf("opencode CLI not working: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	return true, nil
+}
+
+func (r *OpenCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
+	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
+}
+
+func (r *OpenCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
+	// Build command: opencode run "<prompt>"
+	// In non-interactive mode, all permissions are auto-approved.
+	args := []string{"run"}
+
+	if r.Model != "" {
+		args = append(args, "--model", r.Model)
+	}
+
+	args = append(args, prompt)
+
+	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
+	defer cancel()
+
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", args...)
+	cmd.Dir = workDir
+
+	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
+	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
+	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
+
+	start := time.Now()
+	err := cmd.Run()
+	duration := time.Since(start)
+
+	result := &AgentResult{
+		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
+		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
+		Duration: duration,
+	}
+
+	if err != nil {
+		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
+		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
+			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
+		} else {
+			result.ExitCode = -1
+		}
+		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
+		return result, err
+	}
+
+	result.ExitCode = 0
+	return result, nil
+}

> AGENT

Everything looks clean. Here's a summary of all the changes:

**Changes made:**

1. **`entire_plugin.ts`** - Fixed two issues:
   - Replaced deprecated `session.idle` event with `session.status` (checking `status.type === "idle"`)
   - Added `callHookSync()` using `Bun.spawnSync` for turn-end and session-end hooks that fire near process exit, preventing the race condition where `opencode run` exits before async hooks complete

2. **`agent_runner.go`** - Added `OpenCodeRunner` implementation:
   - `AgentNameOpenCode` constant
   - Factory case in `NewAgentRunner`
   - Full runner with `E2E_OPENCODE_MODEL` env var support, `opencode run` command invocation

3. **`testenv.go`** - Added OpenCode-specific test environment setup:
   - Writes `opencode.json` with `"external_directory": "allow"` to work around OpenCode's auto-reject of file writes in non-interactive mode

4. **`mise.toml`** - Added `test:e2e:opencode` task

5. **`scenario_checkpoint_workflows_test.go`** - Minor lint fixes (`assert.Equal` -> `assert.Len`)

**Remaining known issue:** OpenCode has a bug where the model sometimes writes files to incorrect relative paths (path corruption with model identity injected). This is an upstream OpenCode issue, not something we can fix in our codebase.
